30. Lib.Weil (continued)

Lib.Weil.DualPair.Lift

Definition 30.1src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: Space.lift
Let $E$, $n$, $n'$ and $X$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $y$ be a function from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $h \in \GMACRoFEO$. Then $\SpaceliftMACRoCWT {K}{\phi}{y}{h}$ is a complex defined as the Bochner integral of $\SpacequotMeasureMACRoCXI {S}$ and the function that maps $g$ to $\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {g}{h})})\times y (g)$.
The theta lift from S with datum φ: the function h ↦ ∫_{[S]} θ(ω(g, h)φ) y(g) dg on the group of the second space.
Structure 30.2src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: Space.ModerateWeight
Let $E$, $n$ and $n'$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Then $\SpaceModerateWeightMACRoCTL {S}{T}$ is a set.
A continuous function M of moderate growth on the adelic group of S, and a continuous map j to that of T with ht(j g) ≤ C·ht(g) for some C.
Definition 30.3src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: Space.ModerateWeight.groupMap
Let $E$, $n$ and $n'$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SpaceModerateWeightMACRoCTL {S}{T}$. Then $\SpaceModerateWeightgroupMapMACRoCTM {\mathrm{self}}$ is a continuous map of $\GMACRoFEO$ and $\GMACRoFEO$.
Theorem 30.4src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: Space.ModerateWeight.isModerate
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SpaceModerateWeightMACRoCTL {S}{T}$. Then $\SpaceIsModerateMACRoCTE {S}{\SpaceModerateWeightweightFnMACRoCTO {\mathrm{self}}}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎

Theorem 30.6src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: Space.ModerateWeight.height_le
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SpaceModerateWeightMACRoCTL {S}{T}$. Then there exists a real number $C$, such that for all elements $g$ of $\GMACRoFEO$, $\SpaceheightMACRoCWF {T}{\mathrm{Space\_\_ModerateWeight\_\_groupMap\_\_app} (\mathrm{self} , g)}\leq \SpaceheightMACRoCWF {S}{g}\times C$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.7src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: lift_mul_right
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $y$ be a function from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Let $h , k \in \GMACRoFEO$. Then $\SpaceliftMACRoCWT {K}{\phi}{y}{(h \times k)}$ is equal to $\SpaceliftMACRoCWT {K}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {1}{k})})}{y}{h}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Definition 30.8src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.swap
Let $E$, $n$, $n'$ and $X$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Then $\ThetaKernelswapMACRoDBL {K}$ is an element of $\ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{T}{S}$ defined as $\ThetaKernelmkMACRoDBK {(\MonoidHomcompMACRoFDM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{\mathrm{MulEquiv\_\_toMonoidHom} (\mathrm{MulEquiv\_\_prodComm})})}{\ThetaKernelubMACRoDBH {K}}{\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_swap\_\_\_proof\_1} (K)}{\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_swap\_\_\_proof\_2} (K)}$.
The theta kernel for (T, S) obtained from K by exchanging the two groups: the representation (h, g) ↦ ω(g, h) with the same functional θ.
Theorem 30.9src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.theta_rat_mul
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Let $h , \gamma , g \in \GMACRoFEO$ such that $\gamma$ is an element of $\SpaceuMACRoCVH {S}$. Then $\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {(\gamma \times g)}{h})})$ is equal to $\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {g}{h})})$.
Proof.
By 2 lemmas of Mathlib OneMemClass.one_mem, one_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.10src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.isAutomorphic
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Let $L$ be an open subgroup of $\GfMACRoFEM$ such that for all elements $k$ of $\GfMACRoFEM$, if $k$ is an element of $L$, then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {(\ProdmkMACRoDR {1}{k})}{1})}$ is equal to $\phi$. Let $h \in \GMACRoFEO$. Then $\SpaceIsAutomorphicMACRoCSZ {S}{\lambda x_{6} . \mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {x_{6}}{h})})}$.
Proof. By ThetaKernel.theta_rat_mul. ∎
Theorem 30.11src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.moderate_growth
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $X$ is a complete space. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Then there exists a real number $C$, such that there exists a natural number $A$, such that for all elements $g$ and $h_{1}$ of $\GMACRoFEO$, for all elements $\phi$ of $X$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {g}{h_{1}})})}\leq C \times \NormnormMACRoAK {\phi}\times (\SpaceheightMACRoCWF {S}{g}\times \SpaceheightMACRoCWF {T}{h_{1}})^ {A}$.
Moderate growth of a theta kernel for S, T on a Banach space X: there are a real C and a natural number A with |θ(ω(g, h)φ)| ≤ C‖φ‖(ht(g)·ht(h))^A for all g, h and all φ in X. Known result: Theta kernels of unitary dual pairs: functoriality, seesaw, growth, rapid decay.
Proof. By ThetaKernel.swap, ThetaKernel.exists_rat_norm_le_height, ThetaKernel.θ
and 6 lemmas of Mathlib Filter.Eventually.and, ContinuousLinearMap.le_of_opNorm_le, ContinuousLinearMap.le_opNorm_of_le, Filter.Eventually.exists, mul_right_comm, norm_mul_le_of_le
. ∎
Theorem 30.12src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.isModerate
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $X$ is a complete space. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $h_{1} \in \GMACRoFEO$. Then $\SpaceIsModerateMACRoCTE {S}{\lambda x_{6} . \mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {x_{6}}{h_{1}})})}$.
Proof. By ThetaKernel.moderate_growth, ThetaKernel.swap. ∎

Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Then $\SubgroupFixesLeftMACRoCZR {\SpaceuMACRoCVH {T}}{\SpaceliftpartMACRoHBS {K}{\phi}{y}}$.
Proof.
By 1 lemma of Mathlib OneMemClass.one_mem

Theorem 30.14src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.lift_equivariant
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $y$ be a function from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Assume that $\SpaceIsAutomorphicMACRoCSZ {S}{y}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $k , l , h \in \GMACRoFEO$. Then $\SpaceliftMACRoCWT {K}{\phi}{y}{(h \times l)}$ is equal to $\SpaceliftMACRoCWT {K}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {k}{l})})}{(\lambda x_{6} . y (x_{6} \times k))}{h}$.
Proof. By Space.integral_comp_mul_right, ThetaKernel.theta_rat_mul. ∎
Theorem 30.15src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.lift_omega_left
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $y$ be a function from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Assume that $\SpaceIsAutomorphicMACRoCSZ {S}{y}$. Let $k \in \GMACRoFEO$. Then $\SpaceliftpartMACRoHBS {K}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {k}{1})})}{y}$ is equal to $\SpaceliftpartMACRoHBS {K}{\phi}{(\lambda x_{7} . y (x_{7} \times \InvinvMACRoFW {k}))}$.
Proof. By ThetaKernel.ω, ThetaKernel.lift_equivariant. ∎
Theorem 30.16src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.exists_norm_theta_mul_le
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $X$ is a complete space. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $y$ be a function from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Assume that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ}{y}$. Then there exists an element $C$ of $\Real$, such that there exists an element $A$ of $\Natural$, such that for all elements $\phi$ of $X$, for all elements $g$ and $h_{1}$ of $\GMACRoFEO$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {(\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {g}{h_{1}})})\times y (g))}\leq (\SpaceheightMACRoCWF {T}{h_{1}})^ {A}\times \absMACRoP {C}\times \NormnormMACRoAK {\phi}$.
Proof. By ThetaKernel.moderate_growth
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib le_abs_self, norm_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.17src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.integrable_theta_mul
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $X$ is a complete space. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $y$ be a function from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Assume that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ}{y}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $h_{1} \in \GMACRoFEO$. Then $\mathrm{MeasureTheory\_\_Integrable} (\lambda g . \mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {g}{h_{1}})})\times y (g), \SpacequotMeasureMACRoCXI {S})$.
Proof. By Space.IsRapid.integrable, ThetaKernel.isModerate, Space.IsRapid.mul
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Continuous.mul
. ∎
Definition 30.18src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.liftBilin
Let $E$, $n$, $n'$ and $X$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Then $\ThetaKernelliftBilinMACRoDBJ {K}$ is a linear map of $X$ and the set of linear maps of $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\mathrm{Subrepresentation\_\_toSubmodule} (\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ)}$ and the set of functions from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$.
The theta lift (φ, y) ↦ `Space.lift` K φ y as a ℂ-bilinear map from X and rapidly decaying automorphic functions for S to functions on the group of T.
Theorem 30.19src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.exists_clm_lift
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $X$ is a complete space. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $y$ be a function from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Assume that $y$ is an element of $\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ$. Then there exists a function $L$ from elements of $\GMACRoFEO$ to continuous linear maps of $X$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$, such that for all elements $\phi$ of $X$, for all elements $h_{1}$ of $\GMACRoFEO$, $\SpaceliftMACRoCWT {K}{\phi}{y}{h_{1}}$ is equal to $L (h_{1} , \phi)$.
For y automorphic of rapid decay for S and each h in the group of T, the value at h of the theta lift of y is a continuous linear functional of the datum φ.
Proof. By ThetaKernel.ω, strongIntegral, ThetaKernel.integrable_theta_mul, Space.quotMeasure, ThetaKernel.θ, strongIntegral_apply
and 1 lemma of Mathlib mul_comm
. ∎
Theorem 30.20src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.integrable_prod_weightFn
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$, $n'$ and $m$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $X$ is a complete space. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $y$ and $F$ be functions from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Assume that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ}{y}$. Let $u$ be a function from $m$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $U \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{m}{u}$. Let $w \in \SpaceModerateWeightMACRoCTL {U}{T}$. Assume that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ}{F}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Then $\mathrm{MeasureTheory\_\_Integrable}$ holds for the function that maps $p$ to $\HMulhMulappMACRoJO {\SpaceModerateWeightweightFnMACRoCTO {w}}{F}{\ProdfstMACRoDW {p}}\times (\mathrm{ThetaKernel\_\_\_u3b8\_\_\_app} (K , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\ThetaKernelucMACRoDBI {K}}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {\ProdsndMACRoDX {p}}{\mathrm{Space\_\_ModerateWeight\_\_groupMap\_\_app} (w , \ProdfstMACRoDW {p})})})\times y (\ProdsndMACRoDX {p}))$ and the product of $\SpacequotMeasureMACRoCXI {U}$ and $\SpacequotMeasureMACRoCXI {S}$.
Let y and F be automorphic of rapid decay for S and U, and w = (M, j) a `Space.ModerateWeight` from U to T. Then (u, g) ↦ M(u)F(u)θ(ω(g, j u)φ)y(g) is integrable for the product of the quotient measures.
Proof. By ThetaKernel.exists_norm_theta_mul_le, Space.IsRapid.mul
and 7 lemmas of Mathlib le_abs_self, Filter.Eventually.of_forall, norm_mul, Continuous.mul, MeasureTheory.Integrable.of_bound, abs_of_pos, Complex.norm_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.21src/Lib/Weil/DualPair/Lift.lean: ThetaKernel.integral_lift_eq_integral_descent
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ and $n'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $t$ be a function from $n'$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $X$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $X$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $X$ is a complete space. Let $S \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $T \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n'}{t}$. Let $\phi \in X$. Let $y$ and $y'$ be functions from $\GMACRoFEO$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $K \in \ThetaKernelMACRoDBG {X}{S}{T}$. Assume that $y$ is an element of $\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ$. Assume that $y'$ is an element of $\SpacerapidFunctionsMACRoCXJ$. Then the Bochner integral of $\SpacequotMeasureMACRoCXI {T}$ and the function that maps $h_{1}$ to $y' (h_{1})\times \SpaceliftMACRoCWT {K}{\phi}{y}{h_{1}}$ is equal to the Bochner integral of $\SpacequotMeasureMACRoCXI {S}$ and the function that maps $g$ to $y (g)\times \SpaceliftMACRoCWT {\ThetaKernelswapMACRoDBL {K}}{\phi}{y'}{g}$.
The theta lifts in the two directions are transposes: for y, y' automorphic of rapid decay for S, T, ∫_{[T]} y'·(lift of y by φ) = ∫_{[S]} y·(lift of y' by φ along the swapped kernel).
Proof. By ThetaKernel.integrable_prod_weightFn
and 1 lemma of Mathlib MeasureTheory.integral_integral_swap
. ∎

Lib.Weil.Periods.DegreeAndUnipotent

Definition 30.22src/Lib/Weil/Periods/DegreeAndUnipotent.lean: gradedBlocks
Let $R$, $X$, $X'$, $I$ and $X_{1}$ be sets. Let $s , s' \in \Natural$. Let $e' \in \EquivMACRoEB {\mathrm{Sum} (X' , \FinMACRoFDI {s'})}{X_{1}}$. Let $e \in \EquivMACRoEB {\mathrm{Sum} (X , \FinMACRoFDI {s})}{I}$. Let $A$ be a matrix of $X$ and $R$. Let $B$ be a matrix of $\FinMACRoFDI {s}$ and $R$. Then $\gradedBlocksMACRoDXU {e'}{e}{A}{B}$ is a matrix of $I$ and $R$ defined as the submatrix of the inverse of $e'$ and the inverse of $e$.
The block diagonal matrix with block A from X to X' and block B from string to string, reindexed along e and e'.
Definition 30.23src/Lib/Weil/Periods/DegreeAndUnipotent.lean: gradedGram

Definition 30.38src/Lib/Weil/Periods/DegreeAndUnipotent.lean: HasDegreeGe

Definition 30.83src/Lib/Weil/Periods/DegreeOfT.lean: ideleLogVal

For a finite idele x, the integer −ord_w(x): the logarithm of its multiplicative valuation at w, equal to −1 on a uniformiser.

Theorem 30.85src/Lib/Weil/Periods/DegreeOfT.lean: Grading.hasDegreeGe_zero_mul_stringMap_mul_sub

Proof. By reindex_equiv, Grading.indexEquiv
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.sum_eq_zero, Fintype.sum_eq_single

Theorem 30.86src/Lib/Weil/Periods/DegreeOfT.lean: Grading.formAdjoint_stringMap_mul_stringMap
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$, $X$, $X_{1}$ and $X'$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $s \in \Natural$. Let $\epsilon$ be a function from $I$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. For s' = s − 1, 𝖳*𝖳 is the shift `Grading.shift` of Y (X ↦ 0, e_i ↦ c·e_{i+1}, times −N at grade 0 and −Nc at grade 1); if Y, Y' have the same c, 𝖳𝖳* is the shift of Y'.
Proof. By reindex_equiv, Grading.shift, gradedGram, toAdele, Grading.stringReversal, Grading.indexEquiv, Grading.formAdjoint_apply
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Fin.sum_univ_castSucc, ne_of_lt, Fin.forall_fin_succ'

Definition 30.89src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: localCongruence
Let $n$ and $E$ be sets. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $m \in \Natural$. Then $\localCongruenceMACRoEFG {n}{w}{m}$ is a subgroup of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$ defined as $\congruenceGLMACRoDOI {\mathrm{Valuation\_\_leSubmodule} (\mathrm{Valued\_\_v} , \mathrm{WithZero\_\_exp} (\NegnegMACRoFV {\mathrm{succ} (m)}))}{\mathrm{id}}$.
The subgroup of GL_n(O_w) of matrices congruent to 1 modulo 𝔭^(m+1), 𝔭 the maximal ideal of O_w.

. ∎
Theorem 30.91src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: mem_localCongruence_zero
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$. Theorem 30.93src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: pow_pow_mem_localCongruence
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$. Definition 30.94src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: residueOrZero
Let $E$ be a set. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $x_{1} \in \LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}$. Then $\residueOrZeroMACRoEQO {x_{1}}$ is an element of $\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w}$ defined as $\mathrm{dite} (\mathrm{Leq} (\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (x_{1}), 1), \lambda h_{1} . \mathrm{IsLocalRing\_\_residue\_\_app} (\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\LocIntMACRoCCN {w}}{x_{4}})}, x_{1} (h_{1})), \lambda h_{2} . 0)$.
The map from E_w to the residue field at w that reduces integral elements and sends all others to 0.
Definition 30.95src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: residueKer
Let $n$, $E$ and $m$ be sets. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $z$ be a matrix of $n$ and the completion at $w$. Then $\residueKerMACRoEQN {z}$ is a submodule of the set of functions from $n$ to $\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w}$ over $\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w}$ defined as the kernel of $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_mulVecLin}$ applied to the map of $z$ and $\residueOrZeropartMACRoGGU$.
The kernel, in column vectors over the residue field at w, of the entrywise reduction `residueOrZero` of the matrix z.

Theorem 30.98src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: map_residueOrZero_mul
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $m$ be a set. Let $z$ and $y$ be matrixes of $n$ and the completion at $w$ such that for all elements $i_{1}$ of $m$, for all elements $j_{1}$ of $n$, $\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (z (i_{1} , j_{1}))\leq 1$ and for all elements $i$ and $j$ of $n$, $\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (y (i , j))\leq 1$. and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.ext

Theorem 30.100src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: index_stabilizer_line
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $F$ be a finite field. Let $i \in n$. Let $H$ be a subgroup of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{F}$ such that for all elements $g$ of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{F}$, $g$ is an element of $H$, if and only if for all $j_{1} \neq i$ in $n$, $\UnitsvalappMACRoHCH {g}{j_{1}}{i}$ is equal to $0$. Proof.

Theorem 30.109src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: basicDoubleCoset_eq
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$ such that $g$ is an element of $\basicDoubleCosetMACRoDKS {n}{w}{j}$. Then $\basicDoubleCosetMACRoDKS {n}{w}{j}$ is equal to the double coset as an element of $g$.
For every g in the set `basicDoubleCoset` of index j, that set is the double coset GL_n(O_w) g GL_n(O_w).
Proof. By exists_residueKer_mul_eq, finrank_residueKer, mul_inv_mem_Kw_iff, mul_mul_mem_basicDoubleCoset
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Set.ext, DoubleCoset.mem_doubleCoset
. ∎
Theorem 30.110src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: le_card_of_mem_basicDoubleCoset
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$ such that $g$ is an element of $\basicDoubleCosetMACRoDKS {n}{w}{j}$. Then $j \leq \FintypecardMACRoIR {n}$.
Proof. By finrank_residueKer, card_sub_rank. ∎

instance : Fintype (Submodule (ResField w) (n → ResField w))
Theorem 30.112src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: transposeGL_mem_basicDoubleCoset
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$. Then $\transposeGLMACRoEYK {g}$ is an element of $\basicDoubleCosetMACRoDKS {n}{w}{j}$, if and only if $g$ is an element of $\basicDoubleCosetMACRoDKS {n}{w}{j}$.
Proof.
By 1 lemma of Mathlib Iff.and
. ∎
Theorem 30.113src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: isBiInvariant_basicDoubleCoset
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Then $\IsBiInvariantMACRoBFB {\KwMACRoBQJ {n}{w}}{\mathrm{Set\_\_indicator} (\basicDoubleCosetMACRoDKS {n}{w}{j}, 1)}$.
Proof. By basicDoubleCoset_eq, IsBiInvariant, isBiInvariant_doubleCoset, isOpen_Kw, HasFiniteCosets, Kw, basicDoubleCoset
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Set.Nonempty.some_mem, Set.eq_empty_or_nonempty
. ∎
Theorem 30.114src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: heckeFun_basicDoubleCoset_inv
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $A$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$ and $W$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Let $v \in W$ such that $v$ is an element of $\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {\pi}{(\KwMACRoBQJ {n}{w})}{1}$. Then $\mathrm{heckeFun\_\_app}$ applied to $\pi$, $\KwMACRoBQJ {n}{w}$, the function that maps $x_{6}$ to the indicator of $1$ and the inverse of $x_{6}$ and $v$ is equal to $\FinsetSum {(\FinsetFilter {U}{\mathrm{Eq} (\ModulefinrankMACRoFDO {\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w}}{\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {U}}, j)}{\FinsetunivMACRoDZ})}{U_{1}}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{\InvinvMACRoFW {\basicCosetRepMACRoDKR {U_{1}}}})}$.
For v fixed by K = GL_n(O_w), the function g ↦ 1_D(g⁻¹), D = `basicDoubleCoset` j, sends v to ∑_U π(γ_U⁻¹)v, over the j-dimensional subspaces U of k_w^n, with k_w the residue field and γ_U = `basicCosetRep` U.
Proof. By heckeFun_eq_sum, finrank_residueKer, mul_inv_mem_Kw_iff, basicCosetRep, residueKer
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib inv_surjective, Function.Surjective.forall, QuotientGroup.eq, Finset.sum_congr
. ∎
Theorem 30.115src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: heckeGL_eq_sum
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $A$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$ and $W$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Let $v \in W$ such that $v$ is an element of $\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {\pi}{(\KwMACRoBQJ {n}{w})}{1}$. Then $\mathrm{heckeGL\_\_app} (\pi , j , v)$ is equal to $\FinsetSum {(\FinsetFilter {U}{\mathrm{Eq} (\ModulefinrankMACRoFDO {\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w}}{\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {U}}, j)}{\FinsetunivMACRoDZ})}{U_{1}}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{\transposeGLMACRoEYK {\basicCosetRepMACRoDKR {U_{1}}}})}$.
For v fixed by GL_n(O_w) in a representation π of GL_n(E_w), the j-th Hecke operator sends v to ∑_U π(γ_Uᵀ)v, over the j-dimensional subspaces U of k_w^n, with k_w the residue field and γ_U = `basicCosetRep` U.
Proof. By heckeFun_basicDoubleCoset_inv, heckeFun_comp, invTransposeGL, transposeGL_mem_basicDoubleCoset
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib if_congr, MulEquiv.surjective
. ∎
Theorem 30.116src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: heckeGL_dual
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $A$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$ and $W$. Let $j , i \in \Natural$. Let $x_{5}$ be a unit of $\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}$. Let $v \in W$ such that $v$ is an element of $\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {\pi}{(\KwMACRoBQJ {n}{w})}{1}$. Assume that $\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {x_{5}})$ is equal to the exponential map of $\NegnegMACRoFV {1}$. Assume that $\FintypecardMACRoIR {n}= i + j$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_scalar\_\_app} (n , x_{5})}$ is equal to $\mathrm{heckeGL\_\_app} (\pi , j , v)$.
Let ϖ be a uniformiser of E_w, n = i + j, and v fixed by GL_n(O_w). Acting on v by the indicator of the inverse of the double coset of T_i and then by the scalar matrix ϖ gives T_j v.
Proof. By basicDoubleCoset, apply_heckeFun
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib map_inv, Matrix.coe_units_inv, Representation.inv_apply_eq_iff
. ∎
Theorem 30.117src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/HeckeGL.lean: heckeGL_zero
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $A$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$ and $W$. Let $v \in W$ such that $v$ is an element of $\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {\pi}{(\KwMACRoBQJ {n}{w})}{1}$. Then $\mathrm{heckeGL\_\_app} (\pi , 0 , v)$ is equal to $v$.
Proof. By mem_intGL_iff_det, basicCosetRep_spec, heckeGL_eq_sum, transposeGL_mem_basicDoubleCoset
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Finset.filter_eq'
. ∎

Lib.Weil.Construction.FiniteMultiplier

Definition 30.118src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat
Let $B$ be a set. Let $S$ be a subring of $B$. Let $g$ and $u$ be units of $B$. Then $\SatMACRoCPV {S}{g}{u}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {u}$ is an element of $S$, $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {g}\times \UnitsvalMACRoFDS {u}$ is an element of $S$ and there exists an element $l$ of $B$, such that $l$ is an element of $S$ and there exists an element $m$ of $B$, such that $m$ is an element of $S$ and $\EqtMACRoDE {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {u}}}{l + m \times \UnitsvalMACRoFDS {g}}$.
For a subring S and units g, u of a ring: u and gu lie in S and lu + m·gu = 1 for some l, m in S. For matrices this says uΛ = Λ ∩ g⁻¹Λ.
Theorem 30.119src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.inv_mul_mem
Let $B$ be a ring. Let $S$ be a subring of $B$. Let $g$ and $u$ be units of $B$. Let $x_{1} \in B$. Let $p$ be a proposition. Assume that $\SatMACRoCPV {S}{g}{u}$. Assume that both $x_{1}$ is an element of $S$ and $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {g}\times x_{1}$ is an element of $S$ and $p$. Then $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {u}}\times x_{1}$ is an element of $S$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.120src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.map_of_le_comap
Let $B$ and $B'$ be rings. Let $S$ be a subring of $B$. Let $g$ and $u$ be units of $B$ such that $\SatMACRoCPV {S}{g}{u}$. Let $f \in \RingHomMACRoHO {B}{B'}$. Let $S'$ be a subring of $B'$ such that $S \leq \mathrm{Subring\_\_comap} (f , S')$. Then $\SatMACRoCPV {S'}{\mathrm{Units\_\_map\_\_app} (\mathrm{MonoidHom\_\_ofClass} (f), g)}{\mathrm{Units\_\_map\_\_app} (\mathrm{MonoidHom\_\_ofClass} (f), u)}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.121src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.of_mem
Let $B$ be a ring. Let $S$ be a subring of $B$. Let $g$ be a unit of $B$ such that $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {g}$ is an element of $S$. Then $\SatMACRoCPV {S}{g}{1}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.122src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: mem_subring_matrix_iff
Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $B$ be a ring. Let $S$ be a subring of $B$. Let $x_{2}$ be a matrix of $I$ and $B$. Then $x_{2}$ is an element of the matrix of $S$, if and only if for all elements $i$ and $j$ of $I$, $x_{2} (i , j)$ is an element of $S$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.123src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: ideleAbs_locFullIdele
Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $x_{1}$ be a unit of the completion at $w$. Then $\mathrm{ideleAbs\_\_app} (\mathrm{locFullIdele\_\_app} (w , x_{1}))= \NormnormMACRoAK {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {x_{1}}}$.
Proof. By finIdeleAbs_locIdele. ∎
Theorem 30.124src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.exists_saturating
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $E$ is a number field. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Then there exists a unit $u$ of the set of matrixes of $I$ and $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$, such that $\SatMACRoCPV {\mathrm{Subring\_\_matrix} (\udMACRoFEZ {E})}{g}{u}$.
Every invertible matrix g over the finite adeles has an invertible u related to it by `Sat` over integral matrices, the relation expressing uÔ^ι = Ô^ι ∩ g⁻¹Ô^ι.
Proof. By isValuationPair_Ohat, IsIntMatrix, IsValuationPair.exists_isPrimitiveMatrix_mul
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Units.inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_left, Matrix.fromCols_mul_fromRows
. ∎
Definition 30.125src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: saturatingElem
Let $E$ and $I$ be sets. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Then $\saturatingElemMACRoERM {g}$ is an element of $\SubtypeSet {u}{(\SatMACRoCPV {\mathrm{Subring\_\_matrix} (\udMACRoFEZ {E})}{g}{u})}$ defined as $\mathrm{Nonempty\_\_some} (\mathrm{saturatingElem\_\_\_proof\_1} (g))$.
For g in GL_ι(𝔸_f), a chosen u in GL_ι(𝔸_f) with u and gu integral and u⁻¹ = l + mg for some integral matrices l, m (`Sat`).
Theorem 30.126src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.map_evalAt
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $E$ is a number field. Let $u , b \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$ such that $\SatMACRoCPV {\mathrm{Subring\_\_matrix} (\udMACRoFEZ {E})}{b}{u}$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Then $\SatMACRoCPV {\mathrm{Subring\_\_matrix} (\mathrm{ValuationSubring\_\_toSubring} (\LocIntMACRoCCN {w}))}{\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_map\_\_app} (\evalAtMACRoDSU {w}, b)}{\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_map\_\_app} (\evalAtMACRoDSU {w}, u)}$.
Proof. By Ô, evalAt, Sat.map_of_le_comap. ∎
Definition 30.127src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: chiAdelic
Let $E$ be a set. Then $\chiAdelicMACRoDMU$ is a monoid homomorphism from $\UnitsMACRoEL {\udMACRoFEI {E}}$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ defined as $\mathrm{MonoidHom\_\_mk} (\mathrm{OneHom\_\_mk} (\lambda x_{3} . \mathrm{Subtype\_\_val\_\_app} (\SplittingCharucMACRoCYV , x_{3})\times \InvinvMACRoFW {\ComplexofRealMACRoFDV {\RealsqrtMACRoHU {\mathrm{ideleAbs\_\_app} (x_{3})}}}, \mathrm{chiAdelic\_\_\_proof\_3}), \mathrm{chiAdelic\_\_\_proof\_4})$.
The homomorphism x ↦ υ(x)|x|^(−1/2) from the ideles of E to ℂ, with υ the splitting character and |x| the idele norm.
Definition 30.128src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: chiFin
Let $E$ be a set. Then $\chiFinMACRoDMV$ is a monoid homomorphism from $\UnitsMACRoEL {\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ defined as $\MonoidHomcompMACRoFDM {\chiAdelicMACRoDMU}{\finIdeleInclMACRoDUM}$.
The character x ↦ υ(x)|x|^(−1/2) of the finite ideles of E, x being extended by 1 at infinity.
Theorem 30.129src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: chiFin_congr
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $x_{3}$ and $y$ be units of $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$ such that for all nonzero primes $w$ of $\NumberFieldRingOfIntegersMACRoFDD {E}$, $\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (\mathrm{Units\_\_val\_\_app} (x_{3} , w))$ is equal to $\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (\mathrm{Units\_\_val\_\_app} (y , w))$. Then $\mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (x_{3})$ is equal to $\mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (y)$.
Proof. By upsilon_eq_prod_mul_of_v_eq, chiFin, ideleAbs
and 1 lemma of Mathlib NumberField.FinitePlace.norm_def
. ∎
Definition 30.130src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier
Let $E$ and $I$ be sets. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {g}$ is a complex defined as $\mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_det\_\_app} (\saturatingElemMACRoERM {g}))$.
For g in GL_n(𝔸_f), Δ_f(g) = υ(det λ)|det λ|^(−1/2), λ a chosen invertible matrix with λ, gλ integral and lλ + m·gλ = 1, l, m integral.
Theorem 30.131src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.v_det_saturatingElem
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $b \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $x_{3} \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\LocFieldMACRoFEX {w}}$. Let $y \in \UnitsMACRoEL {\mathrm{Matrix} (I , I , \LocFieldMACRoFEX {w})}$. Assume that $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_map\_\_app} (\evalAtMACRoDSU {w}, b)$ is equal to $x_{3}$. Assume that $\SatMACRoCPV {\mathrm{Subring\_\_matrix} (\mathrm{ValuationSubring\_\_toSubring} (\LocIntMACRoCCN {w}))}{x_{3}}{y}$. Then $\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (\mathrm{Matrix\_\_det\_\_app} (\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\saturatingElemMACRoERM {b}}, w))$ is equal to $\mathrm{Valued\_\_v\_\_app} (\MatrixdetMACRoGY {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {y}})$.
Let u be the matrix chosen by `Sat.exists_saturating` with uÔⁿ = Ôⁿ ∩ b⁻¹Ôⁿ. If y satisfies `Sat` over O_w for the component of b at w, then det u at w and det y have the same valuation.
Proof. By mem_intGL, mem_intGL_iff_det, Sat.map_evalAt, saturatingElem, Sat.inv_mul_mem
and 1 lemma of Mathlib eq_of_inv_mul_eq_one
. ∎
Theorem 30.132src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.glMultiplier_eq
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $u , b \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Assume that $\SatMACRoCPV {\mathrm{Subring\_\_matrix} (\udMACRoFEZ {E})}{b}{u}$. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {b}$ is equal to $\mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_det\_\_app} (u))$.
Let b, u be in GL_n(𝔸_f) with u and bu integral and lu + mbu = 1 for some integral matrices l, m. Then Δ_f(b) = χ_f(det u).
Proof. By Sat.map_evalAt, Sat.v_det_saturatingElem, chiFin_congr. ∎
Theorem 30.133src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier_inv
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {\InvinvMACRoFW {g}}$ is equal to $\mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_det\_\_app} (g))\times \glMultiplierMACRoDXP {g}$.
For an invertible matrix g over the finite adeles, Δ_f(g⁻¹) = υ(det g)|det g|^(−1/2) Δ_f(g).
Proof. By Sat, Sat.glMultiplier_eq
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Units.mul_inv_eq_iff_eq_mul, map_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.134src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier_mul_of_mem
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $g , k , k' \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Assume that $k$ is an element of $\intGLMACRoEAZ {I}{\udMACRoFEZ {E}}$. Assume that $k'$ is an element of $\intGLMACRoEAZ {I}{\udMACRoFEZ {E}}$. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {(k \times g \times k')}$ is equal to $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {g}$.
Proof. By mem_intGL, Sat.glMultiplier_eq, Sat.of_mem, glMultiplier_inv, saturatingElem
and 1 lemma of Mathlib MulMemClass.mul_mem
. ∎
Theorem 30.135src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: Sat.glMultiplier_transpose
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $u , b , b' \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Assume that $\SatMACRoCPV {\mathrm{Subring\_\_matrix} (\udMACRoFEZ {E})}{b}{u}$. Assume that $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {b'}$ is equal to the transpose of $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {b}$. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {b'}$ is equal to $\mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_det\_\_app} (u))$.
Proof. By invTransposeGL_mem_intGL, isValuationPair_Ohat, glMultiplier_mul_of_mem, Sat.glMultiplier_eq, invTransposeGL, exists_invTransposeGL_inv_eq
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Units.ext
. ∎
Theorem 30.136src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier_star
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $b \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {\mathrm{Star\_\_star} (b)}$ is equal to $\mathrm{starRingEnd\_\_app} (\ComplexMACRoAM , \glMultiplierMACRoDXP {b})$.
If b' is the conjugate transpose of the invertible finite adelic matrix b, then Δ_f(b') is the complex conjugate of Δ_f(b).
Proof. By Sat.glMultiplier_transpose, 𝔸f, glMultiplier, SplittingChar.υ_star, chiAdelic, star_mem_Ô, Sat.map_of_le_comap, saturatingElem, chiFin
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Complex.inv_eq_conj, Matrix.det_apply'
. ∎
Theorem 30.137src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier_mul
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $b , b' \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Assume that for all finite places $w$ of $E$, $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_map\_\_app} (\evalAtMACRoDSU {w}, b)$ is equal to $1$ or $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_map\_\_app} (\evalAtMACRoDSU {w}, b')$ is equal to $1$. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {(b \times b')}$ is equal to $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {b}\times \glMultiplierMACRoDXP {b'}$.
If b, b′ in GL_n(𝔸_f) are such that at every finite place at least one of them has component 1, then Δ_f(bb′) = Δ_f(b)Δ_f(b′).
Proof. By Sat.map_evalAt, Sat.of_mem, Sat.v_det_saturatingElem, chiFin_congr, saturatingElem
and 1 lemma of Mathlib map_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.138src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier_fromBlocks
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$, $J$ and $n$ be finite types. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Let $g' \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {J}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Let $e \in \EquivMACRoEB {n}{\mathrm{Sum} (I , J)}$. Let $G \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$ such that $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {G}$ is equal to the submatrix of $e$ and $e$. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {G}$ is equal to $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {g}\times \glMultiplierMACRoDXP {g'}$.
Δ_f is multiplicative on blocks: if an invertible finite adelic matrix G is, after reindexing by a bijection e, block diagonal with invertible blocks g and g′, then Δ_f(G) = Δ_f(g)Δ_f(g′).
Proof. By reindex_equiv, blockGL, reindexGL, Sat.glMultiplier_eq, inv_fromBlocks_zero₂₁_of_isUnit_iff', saturatingElem
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib map_mul, eq_add_of_sub_eq
. ∎
Theorem 30.139src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier_star_inv_mul
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $b \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {I}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Then $\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {\mathrm{Star\_\_star} (\InvinvMACRoFW {b})}\times \glMultiplierMACRoDXP {b}$ is equal to $\ComplexofRealMACRoFDV {\NormnormMACRoAK {\glMultiplierMACRoDXP {b}}}^ {2}\times \mathrm{starRingEnd\_\_app} (\ComplexMACRoAM , \mathrm{chiFin\_\_app} (\mathrm{Matrix\_\_GeneralLinearGroup\_\_det\_\_app} (b)))$.
For b, b′ in GL_ι(𝔸_f) with b′ the conjugate transpose of b⁻¹, one has Δ_f(b′)Δ_f(b) = |Δ_f(b)|² · conj χ_f(det b), where χ_f = υ|·|^(−1/2).
Proof. By glMultiplier_inv, glMultiplier_star
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Complex.conj_mul'
. ∎
Theorem 30.140src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: glMultiplier_eq_pow_of_local

Theorem 30.148src/Lib/Weil/Construction/FiniteMultiplier.lean: finMultiplier_eq_of_supportedOn

. ∎

Theorem 30.192src/Lib/Weil/Construction/PairDefs.lean: Space.IsThetaFunctional.comp

Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\SpacedataInnerMACRoCVS {V}}{\SpacevacElemMACRoCXX {V}}$ is equal to the inverse of $\mathrm{starRingEnd\_\_app} (\ComplexMACRoAM , \weilMultiplierMACRoFAX {g})$.
For every g in G, the matrix coefficient ⟨vac, ω(g)vac⟩ is the inverse of the complex conjugate of Δ(g).
Proof. By Heisenberg.IsVacuum.modelInner_vacElem, Heisenberg.IsVacuum.conj_delta_mul_weilRep_vacElem, adelicAction
and 1 lemma of Mathlib eq_inv_of_mul_eq_one_right
. ∎
Theorem 30.225src/Lib/Weil/Construction/PairDefs.lean: Space.θ_ϱ
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. For f in 𝒮(V) and an adelic vector y, θ(ϱ(y)f) is the value f̃(y) of the function on 𝔸^n attached to f.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎

Theorem 30.227src/Lib/Weil/Construction/PairDefs.lean: Space.hasSum_dataInner
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Theorem 30.257src/Lib/Weil/Construction/DataProduct.lean: realSigns_comp_split
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $X_{1}$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Then the composition of $\realSignspartMACRoHBV {E}{s}$ and the composition of the inverse of $\mathrm{Equiv\_\_prodSumDistrib}$ applied to the set of infinite places of $E$, $X_{1}$ and $X$ and the product congruence of the identity of the set of infinite places of $E$ and $e$ is equal to $\mathrm{Sum\_\_elim} (\realSignspartMACRoHBV {E}{s_{1}}, \realSignspartMACRoHBV {E}{s_{2}})$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Definition 30.258src/Lib/Weil/Construction/DataProduct.lean: Space.boxProd
Let $E$, $n$, $X$ and $X_{1}$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $s_{2}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $e \in \EquivMACRoEB {\mathrm{Sum} (X_{1} , X)}{n}$ such that $\FunctioncompMACRoEM {s}{e}$ is equal to $\mathrm{Sum\_\_elim} (s_{1} , s_{2})$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $V_{1} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X_{1}}{s_{1}}$. Let $V_{2} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{2}}$. Then $\SpaceboxProdMACRoCVP {\mathrm{hs}}$ is a continuous linear map of $\udaeMACRoFEV {V_{1}}$ and the set of continuous linear maps of $\udaeMACRoFEV {V_{2}}$ and $\udaeMACRoFEV {V}$ defined as $\HeisenbergOrthogonalSumtmulMACRoBAD {(\HeisenbergOrthogonalSummkMACRoBAC {\mathrm{ContinuousAddEquiv\_\_trans} (\mathrm{ContinuousAddEquiv\_\_symm} (\mathrm{ContinuousAddEquiv\_\_mk'} (\mathrm{Homeomorph\_\_sumArrowHomeomorphProdArrow} , \mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_3})), \mathrm{ContinuousAddEquiv\_\_mk} (\mathrm{AddEquiv\_\_mk} (\mathrm{Equiv\_\_mk} (\lambda x_{30} . \FunctioncompMACRoEM {x_{30}}{\mathrm{Equiv\_\_symm} (e)}, \lambda x_{31} . \FunctioncompMACRoEM {x_{31}}{e}, \mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_4} , \mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_5}), \mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_6}), \mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_7} , \mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_8}))}{\mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_9} (\mathrm{hs})}{\mathrm{Space\_\_boxProd\_\_\_proof\_10} (\mathrm{hs})})}$.
For a sign-compatible splitting of the coordinates, the bounded bilinear map 𝒮(V₁) × 𝒮(V₂) → 𝒮(V), c₁ ⊠ c₂ = ∬ c₁(y₁)c₂(y₂) ϱ(y₁,0)ϱ(0,y₂)vac dy₁dy₂, where ϱ(y)c(x) = β(y,x)c(x−y).

Theorem 30.260src/Lib/Weil/Construction/DataProduct.lean: Space.boxProd_rho

Theorem 30.263src/Lib/Weil/Construction/DataProduct.lean: weilMultiplier_fromBlocks
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $X_{1}$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $s_{2}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $s_{1}$ be a function from $X_{1}$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $e \in \EquivMACRoEB {\mathrm{Sum} (X_{1} , X)}{n}$ such that the composition of $s$ and $e$ is equal to $\mathrm{Sum\_\_elim} (s_{1} , s_{2})$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Let $g , g_{1} , g_{2} \in \GMACRoFEO$ such that the submatrix of $e$ and $e$ is equal to $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_fromBlocks} (\GmatMACRoARJ {g_{1}}, 0 , 0 , \GmatMACRoARJ {g_{2}})$. Then $\EqtMACRoDE {\weilMultiplierMACRoFAX {g}}{\weilMultiplierMACRoFAX {g_{1}}\times \weilMultiplierMACRoFAX {g_{2}}}$.
The multiplier Δ is multiplicative on blocks: if the coordinates split compatibly with the signs and g is block diagonal with blocks g₁, g₂ in the adelic unitary groups of the parts, then Δ(g) = Δ(g₁)Δ(g₂).
Proof. By reindex_equiv, realMultiplier, weilMultiplier, principalMinor_fromBlocks, glMultiplier_fromBlocks, principalMinor_submatrix
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.prod_mul_distrib, mul_mul_mul_comm, map_mul, Matrix.ext_iff
. ∎

Theorem 30.301src/Lib/Weil/Construction/Projections.lean: Space.ext_integrated
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $S \in \wp \mathrm{FinPlace} (E)$. Let $Y$ be a normed additive commutative group such that $Y$ is a normed space over $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $Y$ is a complete space. Let $f \in \udaeMACRoFEV {V}$ such that $\EqtMACRoDE {\mathrm{Space\_\_projOn\_\_app} (V , S , f)}{f}$. Let $L$ be a continuous linear map of $\udaeMACRoFEV {V}$ and $Y$ such that for all multivariate polynomials $P$ of $\ProdMACRoEZ {\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace} (E)}{n}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$, for all functions $y$ from $n$ to $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$, if for all elements $i$ of $n$, for all finite places $w$ of $E$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SetconjClosureMACRoCQG {S}}{w}$, then $\EqtMACRoDE {y (i , w)}{0}$, then $\EqtMACRoDE {L (\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\SpaceintegratedMACRoCWQ {V}}{\mathrm{polyVector\_\_app} (\realSignspartMACRoHBV {E}{s}, P)})}{0}$. Then $\EqtMACRoDE {L (f)}{0}$.
Let P^S f = f (`Space.projOn`), and let L be a bounded linear map from 𝒮(V) to a complex Banach space Y killing v_P ⊗ ϱ_f(y)vac (`Space.finHeisOp`) for all polynomials P and all finite y zero above S ∪ S̄. Then L(f) = 0.
Proof. By Space.ext_of_vacElem, Space.finHeisOp_vacElem, Space.projOn_finHeisOp, denseRange_polyVector, Space.finHeisOp_finHeisOp, Space.exists_rho_integrated_eq, Space.projReal_vacElem
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib ContinuousLinearMap.isClosed_ker, isClosed_property, map_smul, NegMemClass.neg_mem
. ∎

Definition 30.327src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/HyperbolicSpace.lean: IsOrthoIntegral
Let $E$, $n$ and $m$ be sets. Let $M$ be a matrix of $n$ and $\udMACRoFEI {E}$. Then $\IsOrthoIntegralMACRoBLG {M}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all infinite places $w$ of $E$, the product of the conjugate transpose of the map of $M$ and $\evalInfMACRoDSX {w}$ and the map of $M$ and $\evalInfMACRoDSX {w}$ is equal to $1$ and for all elements $i$ of $m$, for all elements $k$ of $n$, $\ProdsndMACRoDX {M (i , k)}$ is an element of $\udMACRoFEZ {E}$.
The condition on an adelic matrix M that M_τ* M_τ = 1 at every infinite place τ and that all entries have integral finite part.

Structure 30.348src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/NameWeilAction.lean: Std

Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {(\SpacenameMACRoCWX {V}{S})}{\Psi}$ is an element of $\SpacefinitePartMACRoCVY {V}$.
For any set S of finite places and any function Ψ on finite adelic vectors, the operator Ψ⊗ named by Ψ at S (`Space.name`) maps the finite part 𝒲_f of 𝒮(V) into itself.
Proof. By Space.rho_finVec_mem_finitePart, Space.finitePart, Space.name, Space.projOn_mem_finitePart, Space.heisenbergComb
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Submodule.sum_smul_mem

Theorem 30.372src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/NameWeilAction.lean: Space.boxProd_name
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$, $X$ and $X_{1}$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $s_{1}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $s_{2}$ be a function from $X_{1}$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $V_{1} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{1}}$. Let $V_{2} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X_{1}}{s_{2}}$. Let $S$ be an element of the power set of the set of finite places of $E$. Let $\Psi$ be a function from functions from $X$ to $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$ to elements of $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $\Psi'$ be a function from functions from $X_{1}$ to $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$ to elements of $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $e \in \EquivMACRoEB {\mathrm{Sum} (X , X_{1})}{n}$ such that $\EqtMACRoDE {\FunctioncompMACRoEM {s}{e}}{\mathrm{Sum\_\_elim} (s_{1} , s_{2})}$. Let $\Theta$ be a function from functions from $n$ to $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$ to elements of $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Assume that $\SetIsConjDisjointMACRoCQF {S}$. Assume that $\IsSchwartzBruhatMACRoBMX {S}{\Psi}$. Assume that $\IsSchwartzBruhatMACRoBMX {S}{\Psi'}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoDE {\Theta}{\lambda x_{8} . \Psi (\FunctioncompMACRoEM {x_{8}}{(\FunctioncompMACRoEM {e}{\SuminlpartMACRoGYC})})\times \Psi' (\FunctioncompMACRoEM {x_{8}}{(\FunctioncompMACRoEM {e}{\SuminrpartMACRoGZR})})}$. Then $\EqtMACRoDE {\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\SpaceboxProdMACRoCVP {\mathrm{hs}}}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {(\SpacenameMACRoCWX {V_{1}}{S})}{\Psi})}}{\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {(\SpacenameMACRoCWX {V}{S})}{\Theta}}$.
Names multiply on vacua: for S disjoint from S̄ and Ψ, Ψ′ Schwartz–Bruhat above S, (Ψ⊗vac) ⊠ (Ψ′⊗vac) = Θ⊗vac, where Θ(x) = Ψ(x₁)Ψ′(x₂) for the two blocks x₁, x₂ of coordinates.
Proof. By reindex_equiv, Space.name_schrodingerAct_boxIndicator, Space.exists_schrodingerComb_eq, Space.boxProd_rho, schrodingerAct
and 6 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.mul_sum, Equiv.sum_comp, AddChar.map_add_eq_mul, mul_add, Finset.sum_fn, Finset.sum_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.373src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/NameWeilAction.lean: Space.name_union
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $S$ and $T$ be elements of the power set of the set of finite places of $E$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $\Psi$ and $\Psi'$ be functions from functions from $n$ to $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$ to complexes such that $\IsSchwartzBruhatMACRoBMX {S}{\Psi}$. Assume that $\SetIsConjDisjointMACRoCQF {\UnionunionMACRoDY {S}{T}}$. Assume that $S$ is disjoint to $T$. Assume that $\IsSchwartzBruhatMACRoBMX {T}{\Psi'}$. Let $f \in \udaeMACRoFEV {V}$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {(\SpacenameMACRoCWX {V}{(\UnionunionMACRoDY {S}{T})})}{(\Psi \times \Psi')}$ is equal to $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {(\SpacenameMACRoCWX {V}{S})}{\Psi}$.
Let S, T be disjoint, with S ∪ T disjoint from its conjugate, and let Ψ, Ψ' be Schwartz–Bruhat on S, T respectively. Then naming ΨΨ' at S ∪ T is naming Ψ' at T followed by Ψ at S: (ΨΨ')⊗f = Ψ⊗(Ψ'⊗f).
Proof. By Space.projOn_ϱ, Space.name_schrodingerAct_boxIndicator, idem, finVec, Space.ϱ_name, Set.conjClosure, Space.projOn_union, boxIndicator, mem_integralBox, Space.exists_schrodingerComb_eq, schrodingerAct, idem_mul_mem_Ô
and 5 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.mul_sum, Set.disjoint_left, DFunLike.ext_iff, ite_and, Finset.sum_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.374src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/NameWeilAction.lean: Space.omega_name
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let S be disjoint from its conjugate, Ψ Schwartz–Bruhat above S, and u = ω(g)(𝟙(g·)⊗f), 𝟙 the indicator of vectors integral at S. Then P^S u = u and ω(g)(Ψ⊗f) = Ψ(g⁻¹·)⊗u, g acting on functions through its finite part.
Proof. By mulVec_finVec, Space.ω, Space.name_schrodingerAct_boxIndicator, Space.map_projOn_eq_const, Space.ϱ, idem, finVec, Space.ϱ_name, Set.conjClosure, Space.projOn, Space.name, boxIndicator, Space.ω_ϱ, Space.exists_schrodingerComb_eq, integralBox_compl_inter, schrodingerAct_boxIndicator, G.mat, IsSchwartzBruhat.comp_mulVec, schrodingerAct

Theorem 30.375src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/NameWeilAction.lean: Space.ω_name_on
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $E$ has Weil data. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Proof. By Space.projOn_ω, rep_apply_apply, detOn, Space.dataInner_name, Space.eq_zero_of_dataInner_self, Space.omega_name, boxIndicator, finMultiplier_eq_of_supportedOn, Space.dataInner_projOn, Space.localInner_boxIndicator_mulVec, finMultiplier_ne_zero, chiFin, Space.name_boxIndicator

Lib.Weil.Construction.ConjugateJetDefs

Definition 30.376src/Lib/Weil/Construction/ConjugateJetDefs.lean: finiteImage
Let $E$ and $I$ be sets. Then $\finiteImageMACRoDUR$ is a linear map of the set of functions from $I$ to $E$ and the set of functions from $I$ to $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$ defined as the left composition of $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {\WeilDataubMACRoDGF}}}{\mathrm{Algebra\_\_linearMap} (E , \udfMACRoFEH {E})}$ and $I$.
The finite image of γ in E^n: the finite adelic vector α⁻¹γ, with α the idele of the Weil data.
Definition 30.377src/Lib/Weil/Construction/ConjugateJetDefs.lean: IsTightFrame
Let $E$ and $I$ be sets. Let $w$ be a function from $I$ to $E$. Let $m$ be a function from $I$ to $\Natural$. Let $M \in \Natural$. Then $\IsTightFrameMACRoBPA {w}{m}{M}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all elements $i$ of $I$, $\mathrm{finiteImage\_\_app} (w , i)$ is an element of $\udMACRoFEZ {E}$ and for all infinite places $\sigma$ and $\tau$ of $E$, $\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i_{1}}{(m (i_{1})\times (\mathrm{PlaceEmbedding\_\_\_u3a6\_\_\_app} (\sigma , w (i_{1}))\times \mathrm{starRingEnd\_\_app} (\ComplexMACRoAM , \mathrm{PlaceEmbedding\_\_\_u3a6\_\_\_app} (\tau , w (i_{1})))))}$ is equal to $\iteMACRoDD {(\EqtMACRoDE {\sigma}{\tau})}{M}{0}$.
The w_i lie in the lattice E ∩ αÔ and, taken m_i times, form a tight frame of length M: ∑ m_i σ(w_i)·conj(τ(w_i)) = Mδ_{στ} for σ, τ in the CM type.
Theorem 30.378src/Lib/Weil/Construction/ConjugateJetDefs.lean: IsTightFrame.repeat_vectors
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Let $w$ be a function from $I$ to $E$. Let $m$ be a function from $I$ to $\Natural$. Let $M \in \Natural$ such that $\IsTightFrameMACRoBPA {w}{m}{M}$. Then $\IsTightFrameMACRoBPA {\lambda x_{4} . w (\SigmafstMACRoFDQ {x_{4}})}{1}{M}$.
Proof. By finiteImage, IsTightFrame
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Fintype.sum_sigma
. ∎
Structure 30.379src/Lib/Weil/Construction/ConjugateJetDefs.lean: Space.VacuumExpansion
Let $E$ and $n$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $\delta \in E$. Let $N \in \Natural$. Let $F \in \udaeMACRoFEV {V}$. Then $\SpaceVacuumExpansionMACRoCVC {V}{\delta}{N}{F}$ is a set.
Orthogonality data for F: N^{2fn} > 0 finite vectors x_j with δx_j integral (f infinite places, rank n), and m ∈ ℕ with ∑_j m·ℓ(ϱ(αx_j)vac)·ϱ(−αx_j)F = N^{2fn}·ℓ(F)·vac for all linear forms ℓ.
Definition 30.380src/Lib/Weil/Construction/ConjugateJetDefs.lean: Space.IsJetConjugate
Let $I$ and $n$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {I}{n}{s}$. Let $\sigma \in \RingHomMACRoHO {\ComplexMACRoAM}{\ComplexMACRoAM}$. Let $\pi$ be a permutation of the set of infinite places of $I$. Let $t , t' \in \ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $f , f' \in \udaeMACRoFEV {V}$. Then $\SpaceIsJetConjugateMACRoCTD {\sigma}{\pi}{t}{t'}{f}{f'}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that $\mathrm{MvPowerSeries\_\_map\_\_app} (\sigma , \mathrm{Space\_\_jetSeries\_\_app} (V , t , f))$ is equal to $\mathrm{MvPowerSeries\_\_rename\_\_app}$ applied to $\mathrm{Prod\_\_map}$ applied to the inverse of $\pi$ and $\mathrm{id}$ and $\mathrm{Space\_\_jetSeries\_\_app} (V , t' , f')$.
The series T_t f with σ applied to its coefficients equals T_{t'} f' with the places of its variables renamed by π⁻¹.
Theorem 30.381src/Lib/Weil/Construction/ConjugateJetDefs.lean: Space.constantCoeff_jetSeries
Let $E$ be a field. Let $I$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Assume that $E$ has Weil data. Let $s$ be a function from $I$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Proof. By Space.jetSeries_apply. ∎

Theorem 30.396src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: Space.Splitting.gram_il
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $p$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $s_{0}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V_{0} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{0}}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SpaceSplittingMACRoCTP {V}{V_{0}}{p}$. Then the product of the conjugate transpose of $\SpaceSplittingiMACRoCUG {\mathrm{self}}$, $\SpaceJMACRoCTK {V}$ and $\SpaceSplittinglMACRoCUN {\mathrm{self}}$ is equal to $0$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.397src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: Space.Splitting.card_eq
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $p$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $s_{0}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V_{0} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{0}}$. Let $\mathrm{self} \in \SpaceSplittingMACRoCTP {V}{V_{0}}{p}$. Then $\FintypecardMACRoIR {n}= \FintypecardMACRoIR {X}+ 2 \times \FintypecardMACRoIR {p}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Definition 30.398src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: hypSum
Let $E$, $X$ and $p$ be sets. Let $s_{0}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V_{0} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{0}}$. Then $\hypSumMACRoDZR {p}{V_{0}}$ is an element of $\SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{\mathrm{Sum} (\mathrm{Sum} (p , p), X)}{\mathrm{Sum\_\_elim} (\hypSignspartMACRoGYG {p}, s_{0})}$ defined as $\SpacesumMACRoCXT {(\hypSpaceMACRoDZQ {E}{p})}{V_{0}}$.
The orthogonal sum of hyperbolic space of rank 2p and V₀.
Definition 30.399src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: splittingOfGram
Let $E$, $n$, $X$ and $p$ be sets. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Assume that the cardinality of $n$ is equal to the sum of the cardinality of $X$ and the product of $2$ and the cardinality of $p$. Then $\splittingOfGramMACRoEUA {M}{h}{c}$ is an element of $\SpaceSplittingMACRoCTP {V}{V_{0}}{p}$.
The splitting of V whose l, k, i are the three column blocks of a matrix M, when M*JM is the Gram matrix of hyperbolic space plus V₀ and ranks agree.

Theorem 30.410src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: Space.Splitting.coord_mul_frame
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $p$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $s_{0}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V_{0} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{0}}$. Let $D \in \SpaceSplittingMACRoCTP {V}{V_{0}}{p}$. Then $\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}\times \SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}$ is equal to $1$, $\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}\times \SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}$ is equal to $0$, $\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}\times \SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}$ is equal to $0$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}\times \SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}}{0}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}\times \SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}}{1}$, $\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}\times \SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}$ is equal to $0$, $\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}\times \SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}$ is equal to $0$, $\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}\times \SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}$ is equal to $0$ and $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}\times \SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}}{1}$.
For a splitting, the three coordinate matrices along k, l and V₀ are dual to the adelic frames k, l, ι: each times its own frame is the identity, and times either other frame is zero.
Proof. By Space.Splitting.kCoord, Space.Splitting.lAdelic, Space.Splitting.iAdelic, Space.Splitting.lCoord, Space.Splitting.kAdelic, Space.Splitting.iCoord, Space.gram_point
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.map_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.411src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: Space.Splitting.conjTranspose_coord
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $p$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $s_{0}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V_{0} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{0}}$. Let $D \in \SpaceSplittingMACRoCTP {V}{V_{0}}{p}$. Then the conjugate transpose of $\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}$ is equal to $\stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s}\times \SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}$, the conjugate transpose of $\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}$ is equal to $\stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s}\times \SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}$ and the conjugate transpose of $\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}$ is equal to $\stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s}\times \SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}\times \stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s_{0}}$.
Proof. By Space.Splitting.kCoord, Space.Splitting.lCoord, Space.Splitting.iCoord. ∎
Theorem 30.412src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: Space.Splitting.coord_mul_frame_mul
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $p$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. Let $s_{0}$ be a function from $X$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V_{0} \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{X}{s_{0}}$. Let $D \in \SpaceSplittingMACRoCTP {V}{V_{0}}{p}$. Let $m$ be a set. Then for all matrixes $X_{1}$ of $m$ and $\udMACRoFEI {E}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}\times (\SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}\times X_{1})}{X_{1}}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}\times (\SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}\times X_{1})}{0}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}\times (\SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}\times X_{1})}{0}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}\times (\SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}\times X_{1})}{0}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}\times (\SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}\times X_{1})}{0}$ and $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}\times (\SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}\times X_{1})}{X_{1}}$, for all matrixes $X_{2}$ of $m$ and $\udMACRoFEI {E}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}\times (\SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}\times X_{2})}{0}$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}\times (\SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}\times X_{2})}{X_{2}}$ and $\EqtMACRoDE {\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}\times (\SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}\times X_{2})}{0}$ and for all matrixes $X_{3}$ of $m$ and $\udMACRoFEI {E}$, the product of the conjugate transpose of $\SpaceSplittingkAdelicMACRoCUL {D}$ and $\stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s}\times X_{3}$ is equal to $\SpaceSplittinglCoordMACRoCUP {D}\times X_{3}$, the product of the conjugate transpose of $\SpaceSplittinglAdelicMACRoCUO {D}$ and $\stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s}\times X_{3}$ is equal to $\SpaceSplittingkCoordMACRoCUM {D}\times X_{3}$ and the product of the conjugate transpose of $\SpaceSplittingiAdelicMACRoCUH {D}$ and $\stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s}\times X_{3}$ is equal to $\stdFormMACRoEUP {E}{s_{0}}\times (\SpaceSplittingiCoordMACRoCUI {D}\times X_{3})$.
For any matrix X, each coordinate matrix (along k, l, V₀) recovers X from its own frame times X and kills the other frames times X. Also k*εX, l*εX, ι*εX are the l-coordinate, the k-coordinate, and ε₀ times the V₀-coordinate of X.
Proof. By Space.Splitting.kCoord, Space.Splitting.lCoord, Space.Splitting.coord_mul_frame, Space.Splitting.iCoord
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Matrix.mul_assoc
. ∎

and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Matrix.mul_eq_one_comm_of_card_eq, Matrix.fromRows_mul_fromCols, Matrix.fromCols_mul_fromRows

Definition 30.414src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: Space.Splitting.ActsOnFrame

Theorem 30.418src/Lib/Weil/MixedModel/ZeroTerm.lean: Space.Splitting.levi_mul_lAdelic
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $n$, $X$ and $p$ be finite types. Let $s$ be a function from $n$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $V \in \SpaceMACRoFEJ {E}{n}{s}$. For a representation π of G_f, the vectors spherical at w: those fixed by all elements of K₀ whose components away from w and w̄ are 1.

Theorem 30.488src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: levelSpace_le_sphericalAt

Theorem 30.495src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: heckeSplit_adjoint
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ and $W'$ be additive commutative groups. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field and $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $A$. Assume that $W'$ is a module over $A$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $\pi'$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W'$. Let $v \in W$. Assume that $\FinPlaceIsSplitMACRoAPN {w}$. Let $j \in \Natural$. Let $\sigma \in \RingHomMACRoHO {A}{A}$. Let $Z$ be an additive commutative monoid such that $Z$ is a module over $A$. Let $B$ be a linear map of $W$ and the set of linear maps of $W'$ and $Z$. Let $v' \in W'$. Assume that $v$ is an element of $\sphericalAtMACRoETY {\pi}{w}$. Assume that $v'$ is an element of $\sphericalAtMACRoETY {\pi'}{w}$. Assume that for all elements $g$ of $\GfMACRoFEM$, for all elements $x_{9}$ of $W$, for all elements $y$ of $W'$, $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{g})}$ is equal to $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{x_{9}}$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{\mathrm{heckeSplit\_\_app} (\pi , \mathrm{hw} , j , v)}$ is equal to $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{v}$.
The adjoint of T_{w,j} is T_{w̄,j}: for w split, v, v' fixed by the part of K₀ at w, w̄, and B a G_f-invariant pairing, σ-semilinear in the first variable, B(T_{w,j}v, v') = B(v, T_{w̄,j}v').
Proof. By IsBiInvariant.heckeFun_adjoint, mem_sphericalAt_iff, isBiInvariant_basicDoubleCoset, FinPlace.IsSplit.star, heckeSplit_star. ∎

Theorem 30.497src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: heckeSplit_comm
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field and $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. For split places w and u and a vector v fixed by the maximal compact groups at w, w̄ and at u, ū, the Hecke operators commute on v: T_{w,i} T_{u,j} v = T_{u,j} T_{w,i} v.
Proof. By IsBiInvariant.heckeFun_adjoint, heckeSplit_mem_levelSpace, mem_sphericalAt_iff, isBiInvariant_basicDoubleCoset, IsBiInvariant.heckeFun_comm, map_heckeSplit, heckeSplit_star
and 1 lemma of Mathlib em'
. ∎
Theorem 30.498src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: IsUnitary.le_span_hecke_eigenvectors

Spectral theorem for Hecke operators: in a unitary representation of G_f, a finite-dimensional subspace U that is spherical at a conjugation-stable set of split places w and stable under all T_{w,j} there is spanned by joint eigenvectors of these operators.

Definition 30.499src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: Weight
Let $E$ be a set. Then $\WeightMACRoDGC {E}$ is a set defined as $\ProdMACRoEZ {(\mathrm{Elem} (\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace} (E))\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (\Natural)\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (\Natural))}{(\mathrm{Elem} (\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace} (E))\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (\Integer))}$.
Weights: at each infinite place of E, the number of columns of each height of a Young diagram, and an integer exponent of the determinant.
Definition 30.500src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: heckePoly
Let $R$ be a set. Let $q , N \in \Natural$. Let $t$ be a function from $\Natural$ to $R$. Then $\heckePolyMACRoDYR {q}{N}{t}$ is a polynomial over $R$ defined as $\FinsetSum {\FinsetrangeMACRoDK {(N + 1)}}{j}{(\PolynomialCappMACRoGI {(\NegnegMACRoFV {1}^ {j}\times q ^ {\NatchooseMACRoGE {j}{2}}\times t (j))}\times \PolynomialXMACRoGJ ^ {(N - j)})}$.
For natural numbers q, N and a sequence t in a commutative ring, the polynomial Σ_{j=0}^{N} (−1)^j q^{j(j−1)/2} t_j X^{N−j}.
Definition 30.501src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: HasHeckePolyAt
Let $E$, $n$ and $M$ be sets. Let $f \in \GfMACRoFEM \rightarrow M \rightarrow \ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$ such that the star of $w$ is distinct from $w$. Let $P$ be a polynomial over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Then $\HasHeckePolyAtMACRoAXS {f}{w}{\mathrm{hw}}{P}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that $f$ is an element of $\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {(\rightRegularMACRoEQY {\ComplexMACRoAM}{\GfMACRoFEM}{(\mathrm{Elem} (M)\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (\ComplexMACRoAM))})}{(\maxCompactAtMACRoEGQ {n}{w})}{1}$ and there exists a function $t$ from natural numbers to complexes, such that $\EqtMACRoDE {P}{\heckePolyMACRoDYR {\FinPlaceqMACRoAPQ {w}}{\FintypecardMACRoIR {n}}{t}}$ and for all natural numbers $j$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\mathrm{heckeSplit\_\_app} (\rightRegularMACRoEQY {\ComplexMACRoAM}{\GfMACRoFEM}{(\mathrm{Elem} (M)\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (\ComplexMACRoAM))}, \mathrm{hw} , j , f)}{\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {t (j)}{f}}$.
At the split place w, K_w fixes f, each Hecke operator T_{w,j} acts on f by a scalar t_j, and P = Σ(−1)^j q_w^{j(j−1)/2} t_j X^{n−j}.
Definition 30.502src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: IsEigenform
Let $E$, $n$ and $M$ be sets. Let $f \in \GfMACRoFEM \rightarrow M \rightarrow \ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $P$ be a function from finite places of $E$ to polynomials over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Then $\IsEigenformMACRoBHN {f}{P}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that $f \neq 0$ and $\FilterEventually {w}{\mathrm{if} (\mathrm{prime} (\FinPlaceqMACRoAPQ {w}), \mathrm{exists} (\mathrm{Neq} (\mathrm{Star\_\_star} (w), w), \lambda \mathrm{hw} . \HasHeckePolyAtMACRoAXS {f}{w}{\mathrm{hw}}{P (w)}))}{\mathrm{Filter\_\_cofinite}}$.
f is an eigenform for the system P: f ≠ 0, and almost all places w of prime norm are split with f of Hecke polynomial P(w) there (`HasHeckePolyAt`).
Definition 30.503src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: IsSphericalOff
Let $E$, $n$ and $M$ be sets. Let $S$ be a finite set of the set of finite places of $E$. Let $f \in \GfMACRoFEM \rightarrow M \rightarrow \ComplexMACRoAM$. f is nonzero, fixed by the integral points trivial at S, and has Hecke polynomial P(w) at every split w with w, w̄ ∉ S.
Definition 30.504src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: HasLabel
Let $E$, $n$ and $M$ be sets. Let $f \in \GfMACRoFEM \rightarrow M \rightarrow \ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$ such that $\mathrm{Star\_\_star} (w)\neq w$. Definition 30.505src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: jumps
Let $E$ be a set. Let $\kappa \in \WeightMACRoDGC {E}$. Let $N \in \Natural$. Let $\sigma \in \RingHomMACRoHO {E}{\ComplexMACRoAM}$. Then $\jumpsMACRoECT {\kappa}{N}{\sigma}$ is a multiset of $\Integer$ defined as $\MultisetmapMACRoIB {(\lambda j . \iteMACRoDD {(\EqtMACRoDE {\sigma}{\PlaceEmbeddinguaMACRoCKZ {\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace\_\_mk} (\sigma)}})}{(N - 1 - (\ProdsndappMACRoHBJ {\kappa}{\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace\_\_mk} (\sigma)}+ \FinsetSum {\mathrm{Finset\_\_Ioo} (j , N)}{h}{(\ProdfstappMACRoGGY {\kappa}{\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace\_\_mk} (\sigma)}{h})}+ (N - 1 - j)))}{(\ProdsndappMACRoHBJ {\kappa}{\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace\_\_mk} (\sigma)}+ \FinsetSum {\mathrm{Finset\_\_Ioo} (j , N)}{h_{1}}{(\ProdfstappMACRoGGY {\kappa}{\mathrm{NumberField\_\_InfinitePlace\_\_mk} (\sigma)}{h_{1}})}+ (N - 1 - j))})}{\mathrm{Multiset\_\_range} (N)}$.
