Definition 30.506src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: heckePolyEval
Let $n$, $W$ and $E$ be sets. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $t$ be a function from $\Natural$ to $W$. Let $X$ be a complex. Then $\heckePolyEvalMACRoDYT {n}{w}{t}{X}$ is an element of $W$ defined as $\FinsetSum {\FinsetrangeMACRoDK {(\FintypecardMACRoIR {n}+ 1)}}{i}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {(\NegnegMACRoFV {X}^ {i}\times \FinPlaceqMACRoAPQ {w}^ {\NatchooseMACRoGE {i}{2}})}{t (i)})}$.
For a sequence t_i in a complex vector space and X ∈ ℂ, the sum ∑_{i=0}^{n} (−X)^i q^{binom(i,2)} t_i, with q the norm of w.
Definition 30.507src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: heckePolyOp
Let $n$, $W$ and $E$ be sets. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$ such that the star of $w$ is distinct from $w$. Let $X$ be a complex. Then $\heckePolyOpMACRoDYU {\pi}{\mathrm{hw}}{X}$ is an end of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $W$ defined as $\heckePolyEvalMACRoDYT {n}{w}{(\heckeSplitpartMACRoGHB {\pi}{\mathrm{hw}})}{X}$.
The Hecke polynomial at w as an operator on π: X ↦ ∑_{i=0}^{n} (−X)^i q^{binom(i,2)} T_{w,i}, with q the norm of w.
Definition 30.508src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: IsSphericalWith
Let $n$, $W$ and $E$ be sets. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$ such that the star of $w$ is distinct from $w$. Let $p$ be a function from complexes to complexes. Let $y \in W$. Then $\IsSphericalWithMACRoBNI {\pi}{\mathrm{hw}}{p}{y}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that $y$ is an element of $\sphericalAtMACRoETY {\pi}{w}$ and for all complexes $T$, $\mathrm{heckePolyOp\_\_app} (\pi , \mathrm{hw} , T , y)$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {p (T)}{y}$.
The vector y is fixed by the part of K₀ above w and w̄, and the Hecke polynomial `heckePolyOp` at each X ∈ ℂ acts on it by p(X).
Theorem 30.509src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: levelSpace_levelOf_apply
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $I$ be a set. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field and $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Let $w$ be a function from elements of $I$ to finite places of $E$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $A$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $H$ be a subgroup of the set of functions from elements $i_{14}$ of $I$ to general linear groups of $n$ over $\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w (i_{14})}$. Let $\chi \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {(\SubtypeSet {x_{14}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {H}{x_{14}})})}{A}$. Let $v \in W$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {\pi}{(\levelOfMACRoEDS {w}{H})}{(\MonoidHomcompMACRoFDM {\chi}{(\levelOfreductionMACRoEDT {w}{H})})}}{v}$. Let $k \in \SubtypeSet {x_{11}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {(\KfMACRoBQE {E}{n})}{x_{11}})}$. Let $x_{5} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{10}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {H}{x_{10}})}$ such that $\mathrm{Kf\_\_reduceFamily\_\_app} (w , k)$ is equal to $x_{5}$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{k}$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\chi (x_{5})}{v}$.
Proof. By mem_levelOf. ∎
Definition 30.510src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: liftResidue
Let $n$, $I$ and $E$ be sets. Let $w$ be a function from elements of $I$ to finite places of $E$. Let $s$ be a function from elements $i$ of $I$ to general linear groups of $n$ over $\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w (i)}$. Then $\liftResidueMACRoEEC {i}$ is an element of $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {(\KfMACRoBQE {E}{n})}{x_{2}})}$ defined as the function that maps $s$ to $\mathrm{Exists\_\_choose} (\mathrm{exists\_reduceFamily\_eq} (i), s)$.
For distinct places w_i, none conjugate to another or itself, a chosen element of K₀ supported above the w_i and their conjugates whose reductions modulo the w_i are s.
Theorem 30.511src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: liftResidue_mem_supportedOn
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $I$ be a set. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field and $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Let $w$ be a function from elements of $I$ to finite places of $E$. Assume that $I$ is finite. Assume that $\IsConjApartMACRoBGL {w}$. Let $s$ be a function from elements $i$ of $I$ to general linear groups of $n$ over $\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w (i)}$. Then $\liftResidueMACRoEEC {s}$ is an element of $\GfsupportedOnMACRoASL {n}{\mathrm{Set\_\_range} (w)}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.512src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: reduceFamily_liftResidue
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $I$ be a set. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field and $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Let $w$ be a function from elements of $I$ to finite places of $E$. Assume that $I$ is finite. Assume that $\IsConjApartMACRoBGL {w}$. Let $s$ be a function from elements $i_{2}$ of $I$ to general linear groups of $n$ over $\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w (i_{2})}$. Then $\mathrm{Kf\_\_reduceFamily\_\_app} (w , \liftResidueMACRoEEC {s})$ is equal to $s$.
Let E be a CM field and w_i finitely many distinct finite places with w̄_i ≠ w_j for all i, j. For s in ∏_i GL_n(k_{w_i}), the element `liftResidue` of K₀ attached to s has reductions modulo the w_i equal to s.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Theorem 30.513src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: apply_liftResidue_eq
Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $I$ be a set. Let $A$ be a commutative ring. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field and $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Let $w$ be a function from elements of $I$ to finite places of $E$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $A$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $\chi \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {(\SubtypeSet {x_{18}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {H}{x_{18}})})}{A}$. Assume that $I$ is finite. Assume that $\IsConjApartMACRoBGL {w}$. Let $v \in \SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {(\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {\pi}{(\levelOfMACRoEDS {w}{H})}{(\MonoidHomcompMACRoFDM {\chi}{(\levelOfreductionMACRoEDT {w}{H})})})}$. Let $k \in \SubtypeSet {x_{15}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {(\KfMACRoBQE {E}{n})}{x_{15}})}$. Then $\EqtMACRoDE {\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{\liftResidueMACRoEEC {s}}}{\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{k}}$.
Proof. By levelSpace_levelOf_apply. ∎
Definition 30.514src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: levelRep
Let $n$, $I$, $A$, $W$, $E$ and $S$ be sets. Let $w$ be a function from elements of $I$ to finite places of $E$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $H$ be a subgroup of the set of functions from elements $i_{23}$ of $I$ to elements of $\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w (i_{23})}}$. Let $\chi \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {(\SubtypeSet {x_{13}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {H}{x_{13}})})}{A}$. Let $\phi \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {S}{(\mathrm{Elem} (I)\rightarrow \mathrm{Elem} (\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{\ResFieldMACRoFEW {w (i_{19})}}))}$ such that for all elements $s$ of $S$, for all elements $x_{8}$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{12}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {H}{x_{12}})}$, there exists an element $\mathrm{hx}$ of $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {H}{(\InvinvMACRoFW {\phi (s)}\times x_{8} \times \phi (s))}$, such that $\EqtMACRoDE {\chi (\mathrm{times} (\InvinvMACRoFW {\phi (s)}\times x_{8} , \phi (s), \mathrm{hx}))}{\chi (x_{8})}$. Then $\levelRepMACRoEDU {\pi}{\chi}{\phi}{h_{4}}$ is a representation of $S$ and $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {(\levelSpaceMACRoEDW {\pi}{(\levelOfMACRoEDS {w}{H})}{(\MonoidHomcompMACRoFDM {\chi}{(\levelOfreductionMACRoEDT {w}{H})})})}$ defined as $\mathrm{MonoidHom\_\_mk}$ applied to $\mathrm{OneHom\_\_mk}$ applied to the function that maps $s$ to the restriction of $\pi (\liftResidueMACRoEEC {\phi (s)})$ and $\mathrm{levelRep\_\_\_proof\_1} (\pi , \chi , \phi , h_{4} , s)$ and $\mathrm{levelRep\_\_\_proof\_3} (\pi , \chi , \phi , h_{4})$ and $\mathrm{levelRep\_\_\_proof\_4} (\pi , \chi , \phi , h_{4})$.
For φ : S → ∏ GL_n(k_{w_i}) normalising H and fixing χ, the representation s ↦ π(`liftResidue` φ(s)) of S on vectors of level `levelOf` w H and character χ.
Theorem 30.515src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: isSphericalWith_of_heckeSplit
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Assume that $E$ is a number field. Assume that $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Then $\IsSphericalWithMACRoBNI {\pi}{\mathrm{hw}}{\heckePolyEvalpartMACRoHAJ {n}{w}{t}}{y}$.
Proof.
By 1 lemma of Mathlib Finset.sum_smul
. ∎

Theorem 30.517src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: map_heckePolyOp
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $W$ and $W'$ be additive commutative groups. Assume that $E$ is a number field. Assume that $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Assume that $W'$ is a module over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $y \in W$. Let $\pi'$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W'$. Assume that the star of $w$ is distinct from $w$. Let $\sigma \in \RingHomMACRoHO {\ComplexMACRoAM}{\ComplexMACRoAM}$. Let $L$ be a linear map of $W$ and $W'$. Let $T \in \ComplexMACRoAM$. Assume that $\mathrm{autoParam}$ holds for the proposition that for all elements $g$ of $\GfMACRoFEM$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\GfsupportedOnMACRoASL {n}{\SingletonsingletonMACRoEW {w}}}{g}$, then $L (\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{g})$ is equal to $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi'}{g}$ and $\mapheckePolyOpautoMACRoEGE$. Then $L (\mathrm{heckePolyOp\_\_app} (\pi , \mathrm{hw} , T , y))$ is equal to $\mathrm{heckePolyOp\_\_app} (\pi' , \mathrm{hw} , \sigma (T), L (y))$.
Proof. By heckePolyOp, map_heckeSplit. ∎
Theorem 30.518src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: heckePolyOp_adjoint
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $W$, $W'$ and $Z$ be additive commutative groups. Assume that $E$ is a number field. Assume that $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Assume that $W$ is a module over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Assume that $W'$ is a module over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$. Let $y \in W$. Let $\pi'$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W'$. Let $\sigma \in \RingHomMACRoHO {\ComplexMACRoAM}{\ComplexMACRoAM}$. Assume that $\FinPlaceIsSplitMACRoAPN {w}$. Assume that $Z$ is a module over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $z \in W'$. Let $B$ be a linear map of $W$ and the set of linear maps of $W'$ and $Z$. Assume that $y$ is an element of $\sphericalAtMACRoETY {\pi}{w}$. Assume that $z$ is an element of $\sphericalAtMACRoETY {\pi'}{w}$. Assume that for all elements $g$ of $\GfMACRoFEM$, for all elements $x_{8}$ of $W$, for all elements $y_{1}$ of $W'$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{(\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\pi}{g})}}{\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{x_{8}}}$. Let $X \in \ComplexMACRoAM$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{\mathrm{heckePolyOp\_\_app} (\pi , \mathrm{hw} , X , y)}$ is equal to $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {B}{y}$.
Let w be split and B a G_f-invariant pairing of π and π', σ-semilinear in the first variable. On vectors spherical at w, the adjoint of the Hecke polynomial H_w(X) is H_{w̄}(σX): B(H_w(X)y, z) = B(y, H_{w̄}(σX)z).
Proof. By heckeSplit_adjoint. ∎
Theorem 30.519src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: sum_coeff_invOfUnit_smul_eq
Let $R$ be a commutative ring. Let $V$ be an additive commutative group. Assume that the set of complexes is an algebra over $R$. Assume that $V$ is a module over the set of complexes. Let $a$ and $b$ be functions from natural numbers to elements of $V$. Let $s$ and $t$ be finite sets of the set of natural numbers. Let $r$ be a natural number. Let $Q$ be a polynomial over $R$ such that the coefficient of $Q$ at $0$ is equal to $1$. Assume that $r$ is an element of $s$. Assume that for all complexes $U$, $\FinsetSum {t}{i_{1}}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {(U ^ {i_{1}})}{b (i_{1})})}$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mathrm{Polynomial\_\_aeval\_\_app} (U , Q)}{(\FinsetSum {s}{i_{2}}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {(U ^ {i_{2}})}{a (i_{2})})})}$. Then $\FinsetSum {t}{i}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\AlgebraalgebraMapappMACRoFDX {\mathrm{PowerSeries\_\_coeff\_\_app} (r , \mathrm{PowerSeries\_\_invOfUnit} (\mathrm{Polynomial\_\_toPowerSeries} (Q), 1)\times \mathrm{PowerSeries\_\_X} ^ {i})}}{b (i)})}$ is equal to $a (r)$.
Let Q be a polynomial over R with Q(0) = 1, r ∈ s. If ∑_{i∈t} U^i b_i = Q(U)∑_{i∈s} U^i a_i for all complex U, then a_r is the coefficient of X^r in the power series Q(X)⁻¹∑_{i∈t} X^i b_i.
Proof.
By 9 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.mul_sum, Module.eval_apply_injective, LinearMap.ext, Polynomial.funext, mul_assoc, Polynomial.eval_finsetSum, map_mul, mul_comm, RingHom.map_mul
. ∎
Theorem 30.520src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: heckeSplit_mem_of_heckePolyOp
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Assume that $E$ is a number field. Assume that $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Assume that $W$ is a module over the set of complexes. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$ such that the star of $w$ is distinct from $w$. Let $N$ be a submodule of the set of ends of the set of complexes and $W$ over the set of complexes such that for all complexes $T$, $\heckePolyOpMACRoDYU {\pi}{\mathrm{hw}}{T}$ is an element of $N$. Let $i \in \Natural$. Then $\heckeSplitMACRoDYV {\pi}{\mathrm{hw}}{i}$ is an element of $N$.
Proof. By sum_coeff_invOfUnit_smul_eq, heckeSplit, le_card_of_mem_basicDoubleCoset
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Eq.ge, Function.mt, Submodule.ker_mkQ
. ∎
Theorem 30.521src/Lib/Weil/Unramified/SphericalHecke.lean: exists_heckeSplit_eq_smul
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ be a finite type. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group. Assume that $E$ is a number field. Assume that $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Assume that $W$ is a module over the set of complexes. Let $\pi$ be a representation of $\GfMACRoFEM$ and $W$. Let $w$ be a finite place of $E$ such that the star of $w$ is distinct from $w$. Let $p$ be a function from complexes to complexes. Then there exists a function $t$ from natural numbers to complexes, such that for all elements $y$ of $W$, if $\IsSphericalWithMACRoBNI {\pi}{\mathrm{hw}}{p}{y}$, then for all natural numbers $i$, $\mathrm{heckeSplit\_\_app} (\pi , \mathrm{hw} , i , y)$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {t (i)}{y}$.
At a split place w, for every p there are scalars t_i such that each vector y with Hecke eigensystem p at w satisfies T_{w,i} y = t_i y for all i.
Proof. By IsSphericalWith, heckeSplit_mem_of_heckePolyOp. ∎
